Deceit at the gate: nine demands, none performed (34:1-31) FROZEN

unit gen_57_deceit_at_the_gate · Genesis 34:1-31 · derive · phase
⇄ verse view — this span in the scroll (trees + morphology)  ·  ☰ all derived units

הַכְזוֹנָה יַעֲשֶׂה אֶת־אֲחוֹתֵנוּ · ha-khe-zona yaase et-achotenu · 'Should he treat our sister like a whore?'

DERIVATION RECORD — freeze-era draft note: DRAFT 2026-08-07 · SOLO ERA unit #11 (Claude coordinator+deriver; external fresh-context adversarial review before freeze per standing law). Span: 34:1-31, the Dinah span, whole chapter: 31 verses · 421 tokens (SNAPSHOT-verified). SNAPSHOT + debut_map. bar C. Conventions: step he: = plain (no accents/meteg; maqqef ־ per snapshot); tree halves = accents kept, slashes stripped; split at etnachta — ALL 31 verses carry one (token-map scan; the strongest-disjunctive fallback stays unused). No ketiv in span. Onkelos BUFFER PENDING. UNIT SHAPE — NINE pushes, ZERO pops, NINE OPEN at the wall: the corpus's LARGEST ALL-OPEN WALL (the prior all-open maximum is seven, held three times: gen_34's Mamre, gen_54's Gilead, gen_55's Peniel; deeper walls exist only in units that also popped — gen_35's 17-pushed/3-popped Sodom board, gen_46's twelve). Every demand in the whole negotiation dies unperformed by its demandee: the performance-shaped answers are the wrong performers' takings (card 1) and the other-root compliance at 34:24 (card 9). The nine, in queue order: (1) 34:4 LET(qach_li(chamor, et_ha_yalda_ha_zot_le_isha)) — Shechem's take-me-this-girl imperative on his father, pushed AFTER the taking already happened: laqach ("take", 3947) walks tok104 = 34:2 va-yiqach (the deed) → tok105 = 34:4 qach (the demand) — deed then demand, CONSECUTIVE career tokens: gen_54's performed-before-demanded class in its starkest form. And the demandee (Hamor) never performs laqach anywhere after; instead the take-verb returns as the AVENGERS' takings — swords (tok110, 34:25), Dinah herself (tok111, 34:26), the spoil (tok112, 34:28): wrong-performer, three times over. OPEN. (2) 34:8 LET(tenu_na(bet_yaaqov, otah_lo_le_isha)) — Hamor's give-her-please imperative on Jacob's house; REFUSED on the letter at 34:14 (lo nukhal... LA-TET, "we cannot... TO GIVE" — the refusal quotes the demand's own verb: gen_35's refused-demand class), then conditionally promised at 34:16 (VE-NATANU, "then we will give" — weqatal duty-content, gen_40's class, not performance): no narrated giving anywhere. OPEN. (3) 34:9 LET(hitchatnu(bet_yaaqov, otanu)) — the intermarry imperative: the verb chatan (2859) conjugates finitely exactly TWICE in the Torah — this invitation and Deut 7:3's prohibition (lo titchaten bam, "you shall not intermarry with them") [see CROWN 3]; never performed. OPEN. (4-6) 34:10 LET(shevu(bet_yaaqov, itanu)) + LET(secharuha(bet_yaaqov, ha_aretz)) + LET(heachazu(bet_yaaqov, ba_aretz)) — the settlement triple: dwell, trade-in-it, take-holdings-in-it — three imperatives in one verse, none performed by the demandee in-span; the hold-verb achaz (270) performs its very NEXT career token at 47:27 (va-yeachazu bah) — in GOSHEN, the wrong land (prospective arm). All three OPEN. (7) 34:12 LET(harbu(aviha_ve_acheha, mohar_u_matan)) — multiply-upon- me-the-bride-price: answered in the OTHER root (the circumcision condition, 34:15), never in silver — and mohar's only other Torah token is Exod 22:16's seduction-law (ke-mohar ha-betulot) [CROWN 4]. OPEN. (8) 34:12 LET(tenu_li(aviha_ve_acheha, et_ha_naara_le_isha)) — Shechem's own give-me imperative doubling his father's card #2: two demanders, one demanded object (gen_54's double-push pattern); same refusal, same non-performance. OPEN. (9) 34:23 CMD-US?(neota(anshe_ha_ir, la_hem)) — the unit's only cohortative push: neota is a true 1cp cohortative (HVNh1cp), Hamor and Shechem exhorting themselves-with-their-townsmen — TIR-033's ?-guard (gen_45's nikhreta, gen_56's nisa-ve-nelekha). The town DOES comply — but by OTHER roots (shama "heed" + mul "circumcise", 34:24); the consent-verb ot (225) itself never returns after the cohortative: gen_45's other-root law (performance in another root never pops) holds the card OPEN — and the consent-verb's whole Torah career is THREE tokens, all inside this negotiation (34:15, 34:22, 34:23): born in the brothers' condition, spent in the townsmen pitch, dead on its own cohortative. OPEN. MOOD RULINGS (the fences). 34:9b-10a's paired imperfects (titnu / tiqchu, "your daughters you-shall-give... our daughters you-shall- take"; teshevu, "with us you-shall-dwell") are OFFER-TERMS, not command-frame imperfects: TIR-028's mandatory ?-guard requires a command frame (gen_33's 17:9 tishmor, God commanding Abraham; gen_55's 32:5/32:20 tomrun/tedabrun, master scripting servants) — a reciprocal exchange proposal between negotiating houses is not one, and the letter supplies its own witness: teshevu (imperfect, 34:10 first half) is RE-ISSUED as shevu (imperative, 34:10 second half) IN THE SAME VERSE — the imperative is the push; an imperfect that pushed independently would double-push the dwell-demand inside one verse. gen_51's yosef-class (imperfect volitive-by-sense → fenced, no push) is the standing precedent. Also fenced: 34:11's eten and 34:21's niqach/niten (self-commits); 34:12's ve-etna (1cs cohortative self-commit, gen_47's 27:41 / gen_52's ashuva class); 34:16's weqatal chain ve-natanu... ve-yashavnu ve-hayinu (duty-content, gen_40); 34:17's conditional protasis+apodosis (ve-im lo tishmu... ve-laqachnu ve-halakhnu) — see the LETTER-NOTE below; 34:21-23's third-person imperfects (ve-yeshvu ×2, ve-yischaru, yeotu — Vqi/VNi by morph, not jussive): retold offer, proposal about third parties, no push. The prestage volitive census (10 volitives: 8 imperatives, 2 cohortatives) is fully accounted: 8 imperative LETs + 1 CMD-US? + 1 fenced 1cs cohortative. LETTER-NOTE — THE APODOSIS THAT FIRES WITH ITS CONDITION UNMET: 34:17 stakes a conditional threat: IF you do not heed us, to be circumcised (protasis) — we take our daughter and go (apodosis, weqatal ve-laqachnu). The protasis FAILS: 34:24 va-yishmu ("they DID heed") and every male is circumcised. Yet the apodosis' taking performs anyway: 34:26 va-yiqchu et-dina ("they TOOK Dinah" — laqach tok111)... va-yetzeu. The stated condition is met by the other side, and the threatened taking happens regardless — the negotiation's letter and its outcome part company at every joint (gen_55's refused-met-condition mismatch, inverted: there the condition was met and the demand still didn't close; here the condition-guard failed and the threat fired anyway). CROWN 1 — THE LAW-VERSE REUNION (Deut 22:21): the unit's frame-words reunite in one statute. nevala ("outrage", 5039) has exactly TWO Torah tokens: 34:7 (nevala asa ve-yisrael — "an outrage he did in-Israel") and Deut 22:21 (asta nevala ve-yisrael) — both riding a do-verb (asa) plus an in-Israel [VERIFIED: full 5039 census; both be-yisrael tokens lemma b/3478]. And in Deut 22:21 the tokens run CONSECUTIVELY: nevala (idx15) → be-yisrael (idx16) → li-zenot (idx17) — the outrage-word of this unit's anger-verse standing immediately beside the whore-root (zanah, 2181) that this unit's LAST WORD debuts: 34:31 ha-khe-zona is zanah's tok1/20; Deut 22:21's li-zenot is tok18/20. The law-verse even keeps the cast: ha-naara (the girl), avi-ha (her father) ×2, anshe ira-h (the men of her city). The unit opens its anger with outrage-in-Israel and closes asking "like a whore?" — and the Torah's stoning-law verse holds both words side by side. CROWN 2 — THE SILENCE ARCHITECTURE: (a) DINAH'S GRAMMAR: the Torah gives Dinah exactly ONE finite verb as grammatical subject — va-tetze, "she went out" (34:1) [VERIFIED: 3fs-finite census of Gen 34 (the only others: yalda = Leah bearing, va-tidbaq/chashqa = his soul, tihye = the land) + her full 8-token name-career (30:21 named by Leah; 46:15 the census-list)]. Every other Dinah-token is object or possessive; she speaks nowhere; every speech in the span belongs to a man. (b) JACOB'S VERB: ve-hecherish (2790 tok2/10) — Jacob "kept silent": the silence-verb's tok1 is 24:21 (the servant silently watching Rebekah — a bride watched into the family; a daughter silenced out of it), and its career cluster is Num 30's vow-law (toks 4-9), where a father's or husband's SILENCE at a woman's vow is what makes the vow BINDING — the verb of Jacob's silence becomes the law's instrument of paternal consent. (c) JACOB'S ONE SPEECH: Jacob speaks once in the span (34:30), and the speech carries EIGHT first-person-singular marks [VERIFIED: 1cs morph census of 34:30 — oti, le-havisheni, va-ani, alay, ve-hikuni, ve-nishmadti, ani, u-veti] and not one word for Dinah. (d) THE UNANSWERED END: 34:31 sets its etnachta on its FIRST TOKEN (va-yomru, "and they said") — the Torah splits a verse at its first word only 12 times [VERIFIED: corpus mark census]; eight of the twelve sit on a say-verb, and this is the ONLY PLURAL one — the whole closing question hangs in the verse's second half, and nothing answers it: the unit ends on an open question standing over nine open cards. CROWN 3 — ONE PEOPLE, TWICE BROKEN: le-am echad ("one people") — the bare phrase am echad occurs exactly THREE times in the Torah [VERIFIED: adjacency census; the one further adjacency, 49:16's am-o ke-achad — "his people, AS ONE OF the tribes" — is a suffixed noun + kaf-preposition, not the phrase]: 11:6 (hen am echad — BABEL, the one-people God disperses) and this unit's 34:16 + 34:22 (the merger clause of the marriage-deal, once in the brothers' deceit-offer, once in Hamor's gate-retelling). The only one-people ever proposed after Babel is this one, and it ends under the sword on day three. Beside it, the intermarry-verb chatan (2859): 19 tokens, but finite exactly TWICE — 34:9's invitation (ve-hitchatnu, imperative) and Deut 7:3's ban (lo titchaten, imperfect) [VERIFIED: full morph census — all 16 middle tokens are participles, Jethro the father-in-law (Exod 3-18, Num 10:29) + one mother-in-law (Deut 27:23)]: the verb is born inviting, and its FINITE life ends at the ban — the lemma's last token (Deut 27:23) is the mother-in-law participle. CROWN 4 — THE STATUTES ALREADY WATCHING: the span's rare words keep landing in the law codes that will regulate its scene. tamei ("defile", 2930) is BORN here — toks 1-3/114 are 34:5, 34:13, 34:27 (heard-defiled, answered-defiled, plundered-because-defiled), then tok4 jumps to Lev 5:3: the verb that will carry the purity codebook debuts on Dinah [VERIFIED: debut_map]. mohar ("bride-price", 4119 tok1/2) → Exod 22:16, the seduction law's ke-mohar ha-betulot, a verse that also holds the refusing FATHER (im maen yemaen aviha) and the give-verb (le-tita-h). chashaq ("long-for", 2836 tok1/7) → Deut 21:11's captive-woman law (ve-chashaqta bah) — beside chafetz ("delight", 2654 tok1/5) → Deut 21:14 (im lo chafatzta bah, the same law's release clause). le-fi charev ("by the mouth of the sword", first of six Torah tokens at 34:26) → Exod 17:13 (Amalek), Num 21:24 (Sihon), Deut 13:16 ×2 (the condemned city), Deut 20:13 (the war law) [VERIFIED: adjacency census]. And nevala + zanah → Deut 22:21 [CROWN 1]. The chapter's vocabulary is a syllabus of its own future statutes. CARE-POINTS (ordinals from debut_map via prestage unless tagged): (1) 34:1 THE EXIT: va-tetze dina bat lea — Dinah introduced as LEAH'S daughter (the narrator's mother-tag; from 34:3 on she is bat yaaqov, "Jacob's daughter", in the desirer's and narrator's mouths); li-reot bi-venot ha-aretz ("to see among the daughters of the land") — the exit-verb frame: yatza tok54/348 = her going-out, tok58 = 34:26 va-yetzeu, the brothers going-out carrying her back [the span's outer bracket]. See CROWN 2a. (2) 34:2 THE TAKING BEFORE THE ASKING: va-yiqach... va-yishkav... va-yeaneha — laqach tok104 [card 1's deed]; shakhav ("lie", 7901 tok14/62; tok16 = 35:22, Reuben and Bilhah — the next family violation is the verb's next narrative token; tok17 = 39:7, Potiphar's wife's imperative); anah ("afflict/violate", 6031 tok5/27) — and tok4 is 31:50, LABAN'S OATH-CONDITION sworn one unit ago: "if you AFFLICT (teane) my daughters" — the afflict-verb's very next token after the oath violates JACOB'S daughter: consecutive career tokens, the oath's word landing on the swearer's own house; career → Exod 1:11-12 (Egypt afflicts Israel), Exod 22:21-22 (the afflict-not laws), Lev 16/23 (afflict-your-souls), Deut 22:24,29 (the violation laws' own verb). (3) 34:3 THE CLEAVE AND THE HEART: va-tidbaq nafsho — davaq ("cleave", 1692 tok4/13): tok1 is 2:24's PATTERN (ve-davaq be-ishto, the leave-and-cleave marriage law) — the marriage-law's cleave-verb cleaves here with no marriage; career → Deut's cleave-to-YHWH cluster. va-yedaber al lev ha-naara ("spoke to the girl's heart") — the comfort-idiom dibber al-lev (speak-verb + AL, "upon", + heart) occurs exactly TWICE in the Torah [VERIFIED: adjacency census of all 67 heart-tokens]: here (Shechem to the girl he violated) and 50:21 (Joseph to the brothers who sold him) — both speakers soothing their own victims; distinct from the inward EL-heart idiom (6:6, 8:21, 24:45 — speech TO one's own heart). (4) 34:4 THE DEMAND AFTER THE DEED [card 1]: qach li et ha-yalda ha-zot — ha-yalda (3207 tok1/1 HAPAX): the feminine child-noun appears once in the Torah, here, in the taker's mouth; the demand rides one verse behind its own performance [UNIT SHAPE (1)]. (5) 34:5 THE DEFILE-VERB IS BORN, AND THE SILENCE: ve-yaaqov shama ki TIME et dina vito — tamei tok1/114 [CROWN 4]; ve-hecherish yaaqov ad boam — the silence [CROWN 2b]. His sons were with the livestock ba-sade ("in the field"); the father hears, and is silent until they come. (6) 34:6 THE FATHER GOES OUT: va-yetze chamor... le-daber ito — Hamor's exit (yatza tok55) to speak WITH him (et-o = ito, the with-preposition 854, not the object-marker); chamor (2544 tok3/11): the name's whole career minus its purchase-debut (33:19, "the sons of Chamor") is this span, and its last token is his death-verse (34:26). (7) 34:7 THE OUTRAGE IN ISRAEL: u-vene yaaqov bau min ha-sade... va-yitatzvu... va-yichar lahem meod — atzav ("grieve", 6087 tok2/3): tok1 is 6:6 — GOD grieved-to-His-heart before the flood; tok2 the brothers; tok3 = 45:5, Joseph's al-teatzvu ("do not grieve") — the grief-verb's whole Torah career is three tokens: flood, Dinah, reconciliation. ki nevala asa VE-YISRAEL ("an outrage he did in-Israel") [CROWN 1] — and be-yisrael (lemma b/3478) is the name ISRAEL's FOURTH token ever: decree (32:29), people (bene yisrael, 32:33), God-title (el-elohe-yisrael, 33:20), and now JURISDICTION — a place a crime can be "in" — four tokens, three units, and the nation-shaped usage precedes any nation. ve-khen lo yease ("and so is not done") — PATTERN-CANDIDATE, NOT OP-IFIED: gnomic negative generalization, but embedded in the ki-clause of the brothers' anger (voice ambiguous: narrator gnomic vs reported outrage) and marked ve-khen, not al-ken; the corpus's nine PATTERNs are unambiguous narrator voice — filed OPEN, owner-gated [see OPEN OBSERVATIONS]. (8) 34:8 THE LONGING SOUL [card 2]: shekhem beni chashqa nafsho be-vitkhem — chashaq tok1/7 [CROWN 4]; tenu na otah lo le-isha — the give-her imperative [UNIT SHAPE (2)]; the give-verb natan (5414) opens its in-span walk: demand (tok86) → their-terms (titnu, tok87) → self-commits (eten 88, ve-etna 89) → the second demand (u-tenu, 90) → the refusal (la-tet, 91) → the conditional promise (ve-natanu, 92) → the retelling (niten, 93) — seven more give-tokens and not one narrated giving; tok94 = 35:4, the next unit: what Jacob's house finally GIVES is the foreign gods and the earrings, buried under Shechem's terebinth. (9) 34:9 THE INTERMARRY INVITATION [card 3; CROWN 3]: ve-hitchatnu itanu — chatan tok1/19, finite-twice crown; titnu/tiqchu — the exchange-terms imperfects, fenced [MOOD RULINGS]. (10) 34:10 THE SETTLEMENT TRIPLE [cards 4-6]: ve-itanu teshevu — the imperfect re-issued as shevu in the same verse [MOOD RULINGS' letter-witness]; ve-ha-aretz tihye li-fenekhem ("the land shall be before you") — Hamor's land-grant echo of the patriarchal formula; shevu u-secharuha ve-heachazu bah — dwell, trade, hold: sachar ("trade", 5503 tok2/5; tok3 = 34:21, the retelling; tok4-5 = 37:28's socharim, the MIDIANITE TRADERS who buy Joseph, and 42:34's tischaru, Joseph's "you may trade" — the trade-verb runs from this offer to the brother-sale and back); achaz tok3/11 → tok4 = 47:27 Goshen [UNIT SHAPE (4-6)]. (11) 34:11-12 GRACE, PRICE, AND THE DOUBLED GIVE [cards 7-8]: emtza chen be-enekhem ("let me find grace in your eyes" — chen tok9/27; the find-grace formula last stood at 33:8,10,15, Jacob to Esau: the appeasement grammar re-used by the violator); harbu alay meod mohar u-matan — mohar tok1/2 [CROWN 4], matan tok1/2 (tok2 = Num 18:11, the priestly gift-word); ve-etna — fenced 1cs cohortative [MOOD RULINGS]; u-tenu li et ha-naara le-isha — the second give-demand [UNIT SHAPE (8)]; ka-asher tomru elay — the whatever-you-say blank check (tomru: relative-clause imperfect, no volitive form). (12) 34:13-14 THE DECEIT AND THE REFUSAL: va-yaanu vene yaaqov... BE-MIRMA — mirma ("deceit", 4820 tok2/2 CLOSES): tok1 is 27:35 — "your brother came BE-MIRMA and took your blessing" (Isaac to Esau, about JACOB) — the deceit-noun's whole Torah career is this father-to-sons inheritance: the father's deceit named at the stolen blessing, the sons' deceit answering their sister's violation; both tokens ride a laqach-story. lo nukhal la-asot ha-davar ha-ze la-tet et achotenu — the refusal quoting the demand's verb [UNIT SHAPE (2)]; le-ish asher lo arla ("to a man who has a foreskin") — arla tok6/10 (toks 1-5 = the Gen 17 covenant cluster; tok7 = Exod 4:25, Zipporah; tok8 = Lev 12:3; tok9 = Lev 19:23, the fruit-tree's foreskin; tok10 = Deut 10:16, the heart's); ki cherpa hiv lanu — cherpa ("reproach", 2781 tok2/2 CLOSES): tok1 is 30:23, Rachel's "God has gathered my reproach" at Joseph's birth — the reproach-noun born at a birth, spent on a foreskin, career complete inside Jacob's household. (13) 34:15-17 THE CONDITION AND THE THREAT: akh be-zot neot lakhem — ot ("consent", 225 tok1/3): the consent-verb born [UNIT SHAPE (9)]; im tihyu khamonu le-himol lakhem kal zakhar — mul ("circumcise", 4135 toks 12-16/21 in-span: the covenant-verb of Gen 17 (toks 1-10) + Isaac's eighth day (tok11) is here weaponized ×5, then resumes at Exod 12:44,48 (Passover), Lev 12:3, Deut 10:16/30:6 (the heart)); kal zakhar ("every male", 2145 toks 11-14/56 in-span) — the every-male word's walk: ark (toks 4-6, Gen 7), covenant (toks 7-10, Gen 17:10-23), Shechem (toks 11-14: circumcised at 34:15,22,24 — killed at 34:25); ve-natanu... ve-hayinu le-am echad — the weqatal promise-chain, fenced [MOOD RULINGS], carrying CROWN 3's one-people clause; 34:17 the conditional counter-threat — see LETTER-NOTE. (14) 34:18-19 DELIGHT WITHOUT DELAY: va-yitvu divrehem — the words were GOOD in their eyes (yatav tok8/38 — the do-good verb of gen_55's unsourced doublet, here approving the deceit); ve-lo echar ha-naar — achar ("delay", 309 tok3/6): tok2 is 32:5, Jacob's va-echar ("I TARRIED" — twenty years); the tarry-verb's next token: Shechem does not tarry a day; ki CHAFETZ be-vat yaaqov — chafetz tok1/5 [CROWN 4]; ve-hu nikhbad mi-kol bet aviv ("most honored of all his father's house") — kaved tok3/23: tok14 = Exod 20:12's KABED et-avikha ("HONOR your father") — the honored-son word en route to the honor-your-father command. (15) 34:20-23 THE GATE PITCH [card 9]: el shaar iram ("to the gate of their city") — the legal locus (shaar tok7/56; 19:1 Lot in Sodom's gate; 22:17/24:60 the seed-shall-possess-the-gate blessings; 23:10,18 the Machpelah purchase); ha-anashim ha-ele SHELEMIM hem itanu ("these men are PEACEABLE with us") — shalem tok3/6: tok1 = 15:16 (the Amorite iniquity "NOT YET COMPLETE" — the four-generations prophecy), tok2 = 33:18 (Jacob arrived SHALEM, whole, at this very city) — consecutive tokens: Jacob whole, the town peaceable, and neither survives the chapter whole; ve-ha-aretz hine rachavat yadayim ("the land is wide-handed") — rachav tok1/2: tok2 = Exod 3:8, eretz tova U-RECHAVA — the land-is-wide word's only other token is God's promised-land description; THE TWO-FACED PITCH: to Jacob's house Hamor said "the land shall be before YOU" (34:10) and Shechem offered mohar; to the town he says "their livestock and their property and all their beasts — are they not OURS?" (34:23, miqnehem ve-qinyanam ve-khal behemtam ha-lo lanu hem) — the property-motive clause the family never hears, as the mohar clause never reaches the town: the deceit-chapter's negotiation is double-tongued on both sides [letter-anchored: token comparison 34:8-12 vs 34:20-23]; akh NEOTA lahem — the CMD-US? push [UNIT SHAPE (9)]. (16) 34:24 THE CONSENT ENACTED IN OTHER ROOTS: va-yishmu... va-yimolu kal zakhar — heed + circumcise, neither the consent-verb: card 9 holds [gen_45's law]. THE GATE-FORMULA INVERTS: kal YOTZE shaar iro ("all who GO OUT of the gate of his city") ×2 in this one verse — against kol BAE shaar iro ("all who COME IN at the gate of his city") ×2 at the Machpelah purchase (23:10,18) [VERIFIED: the formula's only four Genesis tokens] — the witnesses ENTER the gate for Abraham's lawful purchase; the doomed EXIT it for Hamor's deal: same formula, participle flipped, purchase to peril. (17) 34:25-26 DAY THREE, THE SWORDS, THE TAKING BACK: va-yehi va-yom ha-shelishi bi-heotam KOAVIM — koavim ("in pain", 3510 tok1/1 HAPAX: the pain-participle appears once in the Torah, for the circumcised town); shene vene yaaqov shimon ve-levi ACHE DINA ("Simeon and Levi, DINAH'S BROTHERS") — the narrator tags the swordsmen by their sister at the sword-verse; ish charbo — each his sword; va-yavou al ha-ir BETACH — betach ("secure", 983 tok1/7): every later token is the covenant's dwell-SECURELY promise (Lev 25:18,19; 26:5; Deut 12:10; 33:12,28) — the security-word debuts on a city about to fall; va-yahargu kal zakhar — harag tok12/42 (tok16 = 49:6, the deathbed retelling: be-apam HARGU ish); ve-et chamor ve-et shekhem beno hargu LE-FI CHAREV — the mouth-of-the-sword idiom's Torah debut [CROWN 4]; chamor's name-career ends in his death-verse (2544 tok11/11); va-yiqchu et dina mi-bet shekhem va-yetzeu — laqach tok111 takes the demanded girl BACK [card 1's wrong-performer], and yatza tok58 closes the bracket va-tetze opened [care 1]. (18) 34:27-29 THE PLUNDER: bene yaaqov bau al ha-chalalim — chalal ("slain", 2491 tok1/13: the corpse-word debuts here; career → the corpse-defilement laws — Num 19:16,18's tent-of-the-dead, Num 31:8,19, Deut 21's found-slain rite — with two homograph stations, Lev 21:7,14's chalala, "profaned woman", riding the same strong number [OPEN OBSERVATIONS (f)], and two poetry tokens, Num 23:24, Deut 32:42); va-yavozu — bazaz ("plunder", 962 toks 1-2/8 in-span; toks 3-5 = Num 31's Midian spoil, toks 6-7 = the Sihon/Og conquest retellings, Deut 2:35/3:7, tok8 = the war-law, Deut 20:14); asher TIMU achotam — tamei tok3 closes the unit's defile-spine [CROWN 4]; tzonam u-veqaram va-chamorehem — the DONKEYS (chamor-the-noun, 2543 tok7/41) driven off two verses after chamor-the-name died [OPEN OBSERVATIONS]; ve-et kal chelam — chel ("wealth/force", 2428 tok1/17): toks 3-7 = Exod 14-15, PHARAOH'S ARMY at the sea ×5 — the plunder-wealth word becomes the drowned-army word; ve-et kal tapam — taf ("little ones", 2945 tok1/28: career → Num 14:3,31 — "your little ones, whom you said would be prey"); ve-et neshehem SHAVU — shavah ("capture", 7617 tok3/8): tok2 is 31:26, LABAN'S ACCUSATION — "you drove my daughters ki-shevuyot charev, LIKE CAPTIVES OF THE SWORD" — the capture-verb's very next token after the metaphor is Jacob's sons taking actual sword-captives: consecutive career tokens, accusation to enactment, one chapter apart. (19) 34:30 THE EIGHT SELVES [CROWN 2c]: akhartem oti — akhar ("trouble/muddy", 5916 tok1/1 HAPAX); le-havisheni be-yoshev ha-aretz — baash ("stink", 887 tok1/7): tok2 = Exod 5:21, the officers' hivashtem ("you have made our scent STINK in Pharaoh's eyes") — the same causative accusation-form, thrown at Moses and Aaron; ba-kenaani u-va-perizi — the Canaanite-Perizzite PAIR ALONE (no other nations in the verse) occurs exactly TWICE in the Torah [VERIFIED: gentilic census]: 13:7 (the land could not bear Abram and Lot together) and here — both verses where the family's own strife stands exposed before the two resident peoples; va-ani mete mispar ("I being men of number — few") — mete (4962 tok1/7): career is all Deuteronomy, and tok5 is Deut 26:5's FIRST-FRUITS CREDO — bi-mete meat, "with few men he went down to Egypt": the fear-phrase Jacob coins about himself becomes the liturgy's one-line summary of Jacob; mispar (4557 tok1/46 — the count-noun debuts in "few in number"; career → the census formulas); ve-neesfu alay ve-hikuni — asaf ("gather", 622 tok9/50): tok10 is 35:29, va-yeasef — Isaac GATHERED to his people: the hostile gathering Jacob fears and the peaceful gathering his father receives are consecutive career tokens; ve-nishmadti ani u-veti — shamad ("destroy", 8045 tok1/32): the destroy-verb of the covenant-curse chapters (Lev 26:30, Num 33:52, Deut passim) debuts in Jacob's mouth, about himself. (20) 34:31 THE QUESTION NO ONE ANSWERS [CROWN 1, 2d]: va-yomru — etnachta on the first token; ha-khe-zona yaase et achotenu — zanah tok1/20 debuts in the brothers' question; achotenu ("OUR sister") — the sister-noun's in-span quartet alternates voices: narrator achotam ("THEIR sister", 34:13,27) / brothers achotenu ("OUR sister", 34:14,31) — two possessives, four tokens, and the last word of the chapter is the sister. The wall stands: NINE pushed, ZERO popped, one question open above them. REGISTRY 0. TESTS 0 [VERIFIED: zero tov-adjective (2896) tokens in Gen 34]. OPEN OBSERVATIONS (filed, never push for rulings): (a) ve-khen lo yease (34:7) — PATTERN-candidate, not op-ified [care 7]; the first gnomic generalization whose voice is ambiguous between narrator and reported outrage; owner-gated if ever promoted. (b) shekhem (7927) covers both the MAN (11 in-span tokens) and the PLACE (12:6 — Abram's first named stop in the land; 33:18-19; 35:4; 37:12-14) — homograph-class note (el-as-power precedent). (c) chamor the NAME (2544) and chamor the DONKEY-noun (2543) share their consonants; the man dies at 34:26, the city's donkeys are driven off at 34:28 — letter-note only. (d) strong 2790 covers both be-silent (34:5; Num 30) and plow (Deut 22:10's "you shall not PLOW with ox and donkey") — homograph note. (e) 854 (with) vs 853 (object-marker) et-pronouns — this span polishes by lemma (ito/itam/itanu/itkhem vs oti), module-local subs. CROWNS (chain-attested + DB-verified). THE ONE FULL SISTER [G57-01, Minchat Shai + the Ramah]: אחותנו ("shall he make OUR SISTER as a harlot?", 34:31 — the brothers' closing cry) is the Torah's ONE sister-of-ours written full with its vav; the two lean twins are Rebecca's send-off blessing (24:60) and the deceit-speech's own "to give OUR SISTER to one uncircumcised" (34:14). The sister-word is whole only in the verse that refuses her dishonor. THE DECEIT'S ONE LEAN LETTER [G57-02, the Ramah via Minchat Shai]: every circumcise-form in the Torah is written FULL — except ONE lean: להמל in the brothers' false offer, "only in this will we consent... TO BE CIRCUMCISED" (34:15). Abraham's covenant-verb (17:10), the Passover stranger (Exod 12:48), and this chapter's own repeats (34:17, 34:22) all keep their vav (builder-checked): the one spoken in deceit is written one letter short. THE TWO SILENCES [G57-03]: והחרש ("and Jacob KEPT SILENT until they came," 34:5) is written lean of its yod exactly twice in the Torah — the father's silence over Dinah, and the vows-law's silence that lets the woman's vow stand (Num 30:12): the silence over the daughter and the silence that binds, one lean pair. DINAH IN THE BOY'S LETTERS [G57-04]: all three of Dinah's maiden-tokens in this chapter (34:3 twice, 34:12) are written in the bare boy-skeleton הנער and read as the maiden — girl-vowels on boy-letters in the stream, the characterized maiden-law holding (its one written-whole exception remains Deut 22:19) — and the SAME skeleton stands once more in this chapter as an actual boy: Shechem himself, "the YOUNG MAN did not delay" (34:19, boy-vowels, builder-checked). One set of letters carrying the violated maiden and her violator. THE DEFILED SISTER, ONCE [G57-05, Kitzur Baal HaTurim]: טמאו ("they had DEFILED their sister," 34:27) — the word's only Torah token; the Masorah's companions are the defiled sanctuary of Ezekiel and the defiled temple of Psalm 79 (named): every later defilement learned from Shechem's deed. THE GRIEF DAGESHED, IN THE VERSE WITHOUT A DECISION [G57-06]: ויתעצבו ("and the men WERE GRIEVED," 34:7) — the tzadi dageshed as ruled; and the tradition marks this clause one of Scripture's VERSES WITHOUT DECISION (R. Tanchuma's list — does "when they heard" lean backward or forward?): SNAPSHOT's accent stream, which must sing the verse one way, binds it forward — the instrument committing where the tradition holds the question open. THE JOINING STROKES OF THE SWORD-DAY [G57-07]: "the TWO SONS of Jacob, Simeon and Levi" (34:25) — the precise Sephardic books rule maqqef (the joining stroke), NOT the wheel-accent, and SNAPSHOT joins them; "that which was in the city" (34:28) joined bare as ruled: the wheel-vs-stroke question that split at Esau's meeting lands here on the stroke's side. POINTING AS RULED [G57-08]: בהיותם ("WHEN THEY WERE in pain," 34:25) — the he with plain sheva (the silent half-vowel) against the printer's compound: a THIRD guttural witness for the half-vowel law's boundary (after the two ayin stations); בנתיכם ("your daughters") bet dageshed; הכזונה ("as a HARLOT") kaf soft with no stress-stroke per the Michlol — its large-zayin question (one alphabet enlarges it; Minchat Shai sides with the precise books against) filed to the uninstrumented letter-size layer. READINGS (named-only): the Kitzur's pair of בבנות ("among the daughters") with Samson and the hidden-daughter midrash; the הרבו pair with Gilgal; the beast-deed juxtaposition; the ונקומה three-list (Bethel, Judah's pledge, the war); garment-changing before Bethel read on prayer-dress.

# -------------------------- Gen.34.3 · THE_CLEAVE_AND_THE_HEART ------------
